piḷḷit tamiḻk kottu - pākam 1 arumpātti vētavināyakar piḷḷittamiḻ (kuṟippuriyuṭaṉ)

piḷḷit tamiḻk kottu
(kuṟippuriyuṭaṉ)
PILLAI-T-TAMIL-K-KOTTU
(WITH NOTES)
Edited by T. CHANDRASEKHARAN. M.A., L.T.,
Curator, Government Oriental Manuscripts Library, Madras
AND THE STAFF OF THE LIBRARY
Under the orders of the Government of Madras

MADRAS GOVERNMENT ORIENTAL MANUSCRIPTS SERIES No. 50

vi tūttukkuṭi, pākampiriyāvammi piḷḷittamiḻ ....

6. pāṭarūpa pētaṅkaḷ ...... ........
7. karutum cila pāṭaṅkaḷ .......

1. INTRODUCTION:

In the long glorious history of Tamil Literature in the medieval period,
we come across 96 minor works running under the name of Prabandams.
Pillittamil is enjoying a towering and unique position among them. It
usually consists of one hundred stanzas and it is split into ten
sections. Each section is divided into one aspect of childhood. It is
composed in prise of gods, kings and patrons. They form the theme of
the poets who glorify the charm and the beauty of childhood.
The poem opens with a benediction. The child's lisp, the prattle, the
cradle song, its tender clapping, its beckoning unto the moon, all this
is described with great consummate artistic skill of the poet. Perhaps
in Periyālvār, who belonged to the 8th century A.D., we recognize the
spring and source of this species of composition. Subsequent poets
followed in his foot-steps and their poems are cold imitations of the
immortal poet whose songs cannot. but ring across ages.
This edition, published under the name of Pillittamil Kottu, consists
of seven works.
Arumpātti Vēda Vināyakar Pilļaittamil arrests our attention. The
worship of Vinayaka previls not only in India but also in lands beyond
the seas. This worship was introduced during the Pallava reign by the
General Paranjöti after the crushing defeat of Pulikësin A.D. 641-42. We
believe that the devotees of Vināyaka will welcome this work which has a
peculiar charm and grace of its own. This work is based on a palm leaf
manuscript bearing No. R 2677 presented to this library by Sri K.
Shanmugam of Kumarakuppam village in the South Arcot district during
1951-52.
Vaikuntanāthan Pilļaittamil is sung in prise of the deity enshrined in
the temple at Tiruvikuntam. Though the name of the author is not known,
from the references seen in the manuscript, it would be clear that he
was a disciple of one Periya Nambi who lived in Sānthür village. This
work is based on a palm leaf manuscript bearing No. R 441 presented by
Sri Villiputtūr Appu Iyengar during the year 1920-21.
Ragavar Piļļaittamil deals with Ayodhya and her ancient glory and the
renown of Raghu's race. The author of this work is Kulandi Mudaliar of
Kurrālam. This work is based on a palm leaf manuscript bearing No. D 323
deposited in this library.
Tilli Sivakāmi Ammi Pilļaittamil , sung in prise of Cidambaram, is
marked by philosophical discussions. The style is fluent and the diction
dignified; the handling of the theme and the choice and classical
expression occuring in many places in this work are a proof of the
author's full acquaintance with ancient Tamil literature and they reveal
gifted poetic talent of a high order. It is learnt from the work that
the author is an anonymous poet of Tiruccendil. This work is based on a
palm leaf manuscript bearing No. D 324. Manuscripts bearing Nos. R 2860
and 2619 have been of great use in the matter of compiling this work.
The author of the work Puduvi Tiripura Sundari Pilļaittamil is one
Muttukkumaran, whose date is difficult to determine. This work is based
on a palm leaf manuscript bearing No. D 326 deposited in this library.
In Tūttukkuļi Pākampiriyā Ammi Pillittamil we get a vivid description
of a different variety of pearls. This edition is based on a paper
manuscript bearing No. R 590 (b) transcribed during the year 1924-25
from a manuscript belonging to the Mahārāja of Ettiyāpuram, Tirunelvēli
district.
Śivanandan Pillittamil speaks of Sivanandan, the hero of the theme, as
a native of Tiruvárūr. This work is based on a palm leaf manuscript
bearing No.R 2676 presented by Sri K. Shanmugam of Kumarakuppam village
in South Arcot district during 1951-52.
I must express my grateful acknowledgements to:
1. the authorities of Dr. U. V. Swaminatha Aiyar's Library, Adyar, for
making their manuscript accessible to me for comparison,
2. the owners of the manuscripts mentioned above who presented them to
the Government Oriental Manuscripts Library,
3. Sri S. Sachidanandam Pilli, B.A.L.T., Retired District Educational
Officer and Dr. M. A. Dori Rangaswamy, M.A., M.O.L., Ph.D., Senior
Lecturer in Tamil, University of Madras, for throwing light on some of
the obscure tenets and doctrines of Saiva philosophy and
4. Vidván B. R. Purushothama Naidu, Junior Lecturer in Tamil, University
of Madras, for offering valuable suggestions and interpretations of
certin obscure lines in the text.
My thanks are also due to Srīmāns E. B. Venu gopal, V. S. Krishnan and
M. Pasupathi, Tamil Pandits of this Library, who prepared the press copy
and carefully corrected the proofs and to the Rathnam Press for their
willing co-operation.
Madras,       T. CHANDRASEKHARAN,
12–2–1956       Curator, Govt. Oriental Mss., Library
-------------------

2. aṇinturi

iṉimiyum meṉmiyum kaṉṉimiyum peṟṟa coṟkaḷ nantamiḻ moḻiyil amintu pāṭṭukkaḷākat
tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. taṇṭamiḻ vallunar maṅkalac coṟkaḷāl pōṟṟuvatum ulōpikaḷi naccuc coṟkaḷāl
tūṟṟuvatumuṇṭu. eṉavē tamiḻc coṟkaḷil cila uṇarcci nirampiyaṉa; cila pakti
nirampiyaṉa; iṉṉuñ cila kaḻipēriṉpam payappaṉa. nantikkalampakam ōr vacikkavi. tamiḻil
pukaḻ nirampiya iṉpam payakkum centamiḻp pāṭalkaḷi avāviṉar aracar, upakārikaḷ
mutaliyōr. appāṭalkaḷilellām iṉimic cuviyip piratipalippatil īṭaṟṟu viḷaṅkuvatu
piḷḷikkavip pāṭṭē. iḷamip paruvam oruvarkkuk kaṭicivari iruppiṉ evvāṟu varavēṟṟu
iṉpamaṉupavippārō avvitam varavēṟṟu iṉpam nukarappaṭuvatu piḷḷittamiḻeṉiṉ
mikiyākātu. kuḻavip paruvam mācaṟṟa nalleṇṇaṅkaḷiyum avveṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ vāyilākak
kaḷaṅkamaṟṟa tūya aṉpiyum peṟṟut tikaḻtalāl avveṇṇam, aṉpu ākiyaṉa peṟa
virumpupavarum, veḷiyiṭa virumpupavarum iṭiyaṟā iṉpattil tiḷikka virumpupavarum
piḷḷit tamiḻp pāṭṭukkaḷil maṉam oṉṟip paḻakuvatu avaciyamākum.
''makkaṇmey ............... cevikku" eṉṟa teyvappulamit tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār
vākkuppaṭi tīṇṭal uṭaṟkiṉpamum, kēṭṭal cevikkiṉpamum viḷivittalip pōṉṟu
iḷamicciṟārkaḷ āṭum viḷiyāṭṭum kaṇṇukkiṉimi tarum eṉpatu pōtarukiṟatu. “kuḻavi
taḷarnaṭiyik kāṇṭal iṉitē........'' eṉṟār iṉiyavi nāṟ patiṉum. ikkottiṉ 566-ām
pāṭṭiṉum “kātukkiṉituṉ maḻali yañcol kaṇṇukkiṉituṉ kāṭciyiṉpam kaṉivāyk
kiṉitāyt kavuṇerittuk kaṉṉantaṉi muttuvatiṉpam.... pētikkiṉiyim poṟik kiṉpam
piḷḷikkaṉiyaṉṟiyu muṇṭō!" eṉṉumpōtu piḷḷikkaṉi evvita iṉpamum taravallatu eṉak
kūṟuvatu tavira nāveḻavilli. itaṟkākavaṉṟō pāṇṭiyaṉ aṟivuṭi nampiyum “paṭippup pala
paṭittu .......... payakkuṟiyillittām vāḻum nāḷē,” eṉṟāṉ puṟanāṉūṟṟiṉum.
maṟṟum "kuḻaliṉ yāḻi ṉamutiṟ kuḻitta coṉ, maḻali taṅkaṇ makārvayiṟ kēṭṭalāṟ, ṟaḻiyu
muḷḷamey taṉṉit toṭiṟpuṟaṅ, kuḻaviyiṉpaṅ kuṟittator tōṟṟamē.” eṉṟār karuṇīka
purāṇattiṉum. tammiyātaritta araca vaḷḷalkaḷ, pirapukkaḷ ākiyōriṭattu aṉpuṭaṉ paḻakum
pulavarkaḷ avarkaḷi avvaṉpiṟkiṉiya kaḷḷaṅ kapaṭamaṟṟa piḷḷimip paruvamuṭiyarāyk kaṟpaṉi
ceytu āṇpālāṉāl āṇkuḻavikaḷiṉ viḷiyāṭṭu vakikaḷiyum peṇpālāṉāl peṇkuḻavikaḷiṉ
viḷiyāṭṭu vakikaḷiyum amittu iṉiya koñcum kaṉṉit tamiḻilē iṉpac cuvi naṉi coṭṭac
coṭṭak kāppu mutaliya 10-paruvaṅkaṭku ēṟpa ceyalkaḷi vakuttuk kūṟi āciriya
viruttappāvāl amittaṉar. aracar, upakārikaḷ ākiyōr iṅṅaṉam kēṭṭuk kaḻimakiḻvu peṟṟa
piṉṉar pulavarkaṭku vēṇṭiya vēṇṭiyāṅku aḷittaṉar. itaṉālaṉṟō 'ītalicipaṭa
vāḻtal........ .... yuyirkku'' eṉṟum "pulavarpāṭum pukaḻuṭiyōr.......
vāṉavūrti.......” eṉṟum kūṟiṉar.
tolkāppiyattil pāṭāṇṭiṇiyuḷ "kuḻavi maruṅkiṉum kiḻavatākum'' eṉṟavāṟu kāmap
pakutiyiṉpāṟpaṭṭu makkaṭkuḻavikkuriyatāka aracariyum ēṉiyōriyum pāṭum pulavar
"kuḻantiyum teyvamum koṇṭāṭum iṭattilē" eṉṟavāṟu akkuḻaviyōṭu oṟṟumiyuṭiya
teyvattukkum uriyatāka ivvāṟu pāṭuvar.
piḷḷittamiḻp pāṭṭil kālattāl muṟpaṭṭatu periyāḻvār iyaṟṟiya piḷḷikkaviyākum.
avar piḷḷikkaviyeṉat taṉiyākap pāṭāmal tam tirumoḻiyil kaṇṇaṉiṉ kuḻavip paruvattiyē
mukkiyamāṉa pakutiyākap pāṭiyuḷḷār. piṟkālattiṟṟāṉ piḷḷik kaviyeṉat taṉippirapanta
muṟi tōṉṟiyatu. periyāḻvār ki. pi. 769-770-kku muṉpiruntavar eṉa
varalāṟṟāciriyar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. pakaḻikkūttar pāṭiya tiruccentūr murukaṉ
piḷḷittamiḻ, antakak kavi vīrarākavar pāṭiya cēyūr murukaṉ piḷḷittamiḻ, vēppantūrār
pāṭiya aḻakar piḷḷittamiḻ ākiyaṉa paḻami mikkavi.
ittakimi vāynta piḷḷikkavi tamiḻilaminta 96 pirapanta vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu, pirapantam
vaṭacol. pira-mikunta, pantam kaṭṭu; ovvoru pirapantamum yātāmoru kaṭṭiṉāl
kaṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. kalampakam 18- tuṟikaḷāṟ kaṭṭappaṭutal pōṉṟu piḷḷittamiḻum pattup
paruvaṅkaḷāṟ kaṭṭappaṭukiṟatu.
iḵtu āṇpāṟ piḷḷittamiḻ, peṇpāṟ piḷḷittamiḻ eṉa iruvakittu; āṇpāṟ piḷḷittamiḻ
kāppu mutal ciṟutēr īṟākavum, peṇpāṟ piḷḷittamiḻ āṇpāṟ piḷḷittamiḻiṉ iṟuti
mūṉṟu paruvaṅkaṭkup patil ammāṉi, nīrāṭal, ūcal peṟṟum varum. kāppu, tāl, ceṅkīri
eṉa muṟi māṟiyum varumeṉpar. ovvoru paruvamum 10 - pāṭṭukkaḷ peṟutaliyalpu;
paḻaṉip piḷḷit tamiḻ pōṉṟaṉa kuṟintu vantaṉa. ivikaḷiṉilakkaṇatti ila - viḷa. pāṭṭiyal
46, 47-vatu cūttiraṅkaḷāl uṇaralām. ikkavi 3- ām mātam toṭaṅki 21-ām mātam vari
oṟṟitta mātamākiya 10 - mātaṅkaḷiṉum kēṭpikkappaṭum. aṉṟiyum 5-ām āṇṭiṉum 7-ām
āṇṭiṉum kēṭpiṉum iḻukkātu. "muṟitaru mūṉṟāti mūvēḻīṟāntiṅka, ḷaṟikanilam pattu
māṇṭintē....................' (ve. pā. cey. 7) eṉpataṉiyum, "piḷḷippāṭṭē
teḷḷitiṟ kiḷappiṉ, mūṉṟu mutalā mūvēḻaḷavum āṉṟa tiṅkaḷiṉ aṟikuvar niliyē'',
"oṉṟu mutaliyāṇṭōtiṉum variyār'' (paṉ. pāṭṭiyal) eṉpataṉiyum ōrka.
1. kāppupparuvam:- kāppu - kāval, kāppuk kaviyik kuṟippatāl toḻilāku peyar.
itu 2-ām mātattil nikaḻvatu; 3-ām mātameṉpar veṇpā pāṭṭiyaluḷ. tirumāl mutaliya
kaṭavuḷar pāṭṭuṭittalivarākiya kuḻaviyip pātukākka vēṇṭumeṉṟu amippatu
kāppupparuvam; tirumāl kāvaṟkaṭavuḷātalāl mutaṟpāṭṭu avarip paṟṟiyatāka irukkum.
kuḻavi piṟanta ēḻāvatu nāḷ kāppiṭaleṉum ulaka vaḻakku nōkki iḵtu ilakkiya vaḻakkilum
amikkappaṭṭatu.
2. ceṅkīripparuvam : - kīr - col, icceyal 5-ām mātattil nikaḻum. poruḷ teriyāta
oliyi eḻuppum paruvamākum itu. ipparuvattil pacciḷaṅ kuḻavikaḷ 'ṅka', ṅka' eṉak
kūṟak kēṭṭut tāymārum piṟarum makiḻvar. ivvoli maḻaliyik kāṭṭilum iṉimi
mikkatu; iḷamiyāṉatu. maḻaliyi oru vakiyiṟ poruḷ ceyyiṉum ipparuvap pēccu oli
māttiramāyt tōṉṟalāl uṇartalaritu. yāppil toṭikaḷ carivarap poruntāta oṉṟic
centoṭi eṉṟa pāṉmipōl collukkuriya amitiyaṟṟa itaṉiyum 'ceṅkīr' eṉṟaṉarpōlum.
ceṅkīri eṉpataṉi oru nirutya vicēṭameṉak kūṟuvar periyavāccāṉ piḷḷi.
ceṅkīriyeṉpataṉ coṟporuḷ muṉṉar viḷakkappaṭṭatāyiṉum ceṅkīriyāṭukaveṉum vaḻakki
nōkkiṉ kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟu koḷḷal vēṇṭum eṉpatu pulaṉākum.
"...karuṇiyoṭu meḷḷak kaviḻntiruki yūṉṟimeṉ
kamalamuka mēṟeṭuttut
teyvat tamiḻp pulavar pōṟṟutaru maccelvi
ceṅkīri yāṭiyaruḷē....'" (putuvi. tiri. cuntari piḷ:)
“malkāntu mellak kaviḻntu viḷiyāṭavē
maṉṉuyirkaḷ yāvumāṭa
virutāḷu mūṉṟiyiru kiyumūṉ ṟittali
yeṭuttut tavaḻntiṭavumē....” (civakāmi. piḷ).
ivikaḷiṉpaṭi irukālkaḷiyum irukikaḷiyum nilattilūṉṟi mukatti mēl
nimirttiyāṭutal ipparuvac ceyki.
3. tālapparuvam:- tāl - tālu, nākku; tālāṭṭu - ōr vaki nāvacippu. atu
ākupeyarāy acceyalōṭu uṭaṉikaḻum pāṭṭiyumuṇarttiṟṟu. tālāṭṭeṉpatu tāleṉa
maruviṟṟu. tālāṭṭik kavaṉikkum paruvameṉpar. itu ēḻām mātam nikaḻum; eṭṭām
mātameṉpar piṅkalantiyil. kuḻavikaḷit tāyar tam maṭittalattum, maṇittaviciṉum, maṇit
toṭṭiliṉum tuyilac ceyvaṟkākap pāṭṭup pāṭutal vaḻakkam. tivviyap pirapantaṅkaḷil
tālāṭṭeṉat taṉittup pāṭiyuḷḷa pāṭaṟṟokutikaḷik kāṇalām. ceṅkīri yāṭiyaruḷeṉṉum
aṭukku ceṅkō ceṅkīriyeṉa viṇintum 'ceṅkīri'yeṉat taṉittum marīi niṉṟāṟpōla
tālāṭṭiyēl tālāṭṭiyēl eṉṉum aṭukkuttoṭar tālō tālēlō eṉaviṇintum tāleṉat
taṉittum marīiyiṟṟu eṉpar īcāṉya maṭam irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷ.
4. cappāṇipparuvam: - sa: pāṇi - cappāṇi, sa:- kūṭa. pāṇi-ki cappāṇi koṭṭal -
kiyōṭu ki cērttukkoṭṭutal. itu 9-ām mātam nikaḻum. iṅṅaṉam cappāṇi koṭṭumāṟu
kuḻantiyiṭam kūṟuvatākap pāṭappaṭuvatu ipparuvam. nam vīṭṭilum tāyar mutaliyōr,
"tattāṅki tattāṅki talimēlē yeṉṉāṭi" eṉṟu kūṟik kittaṭṭac colvatik
kaṇṭirukkalām. kālvaliyiṉṟik kivali koṇṭu nakarntu celluṅ kuḻantikaḷic ‘cappāṇi'
yeṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakki yuṇarka.
5. muttapparuvam:- muttam - vāyitaḻ (piṅ); aṉpiṟkaṟikuṟiyāka oruvaki yoliyuṇṭāka
utaṭukaḷāṟ paricittal (cūṭā), kuḻantiyi muttan tāveṉṟu tāyarum piṟarum
vēṇṭuvatākak kūṟutal marapu. itu 11-ām mātam nikaḻum. muttam eṉum col muttiṉiyum
kuṟittaliṉ muttapparuvattil pulavarkaḷ piṟaviṭaṅkaḷil kiṭikkum palavaki muttaṅkaḷiṉum
keṭāta niṉ vāy muttamē ciṟantatākaliṉ ataṉittaruka'' eṉṟu amittal vaḻakkam. ivvāṟu
iṟuti 4-piḷḷit tamiḻilum parakkak kāṇalām. civāṉantaṉ piḷḷit tamiḻil mēlum
10-vaki mutti vakikaḷik kūṟi avi \kaḷik kāṭṭilum ''aruḷoṭu puṇarnta muttiyē
ciṟantatu" eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu nōkkattakkatu.
6. varukipparuvam: - itu vārāṉip paruvam eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappeṟum. vārāṉi - varuki,
āṉi - toḻiṟpeyar vikuti. taḷarnaṭi yiṭṭuvarum kuḻaviyit tōḻiyar vāveṉṟu
aḻippatākak kūṟuvatu ipparuvac ceyti. itu 12-ām mātattiṟ kiḷattuvatu. kuḻavikaḷi
aṇi, uṭi mutaliyaṉa aṇiyak kūppiṭuvatu muṟi. cila kuḻavikaḷ aṭam ceytu avikaḷiyaṇiya
maṟukkum; itaṉi etirmaṟi kuṟippuṇarttal mūlam camātāṉap paṭuttalāmeṉpar maṉa nūlaṟiñar.
itaṉpaṭi 596-ām pāṭṭu amintirukkiṟatu.
7. ampulip paruvam: - ampuli -cantiraṉ. cantiraṉik kuḻantiyōṭu viḷiyāṭa vāveṉṟu
aḻittalik kūṟuvatu; 16-ām mātattil cantiraṉi yaḻippatu nikaḻum. 18-ām mātameṉpar
piṅkalantiyil. irāmar kuḻavip paruvattil cantiraṉik kaṇṭu arukil varac coṉṉapōtu
yārālum muṭiyāmaṟ pōṉatāl aḻuki mika, iṟutiyil cumantiraṉ kaṇṇāṭiyil cantiraṉik
kāṭṭic camātāṉap paṭuttiyati yārēyaṟiyār ! ipparuvam ēṉiya paruvaṅkaḷiṉum
ariyateṉpār "piḷḷik kavikkampuli puliyām" eṉpatāl viḷaṅkum. ampuliyi
yaḻikkiyil cāma, pēta, tāṉa, taṇṭa vakiyāl muṟiyē cantiraṉukkum pāṭṭuṭit
talivarkkum oppuk kūṟutalum, vēṟṟumi kūṟalum, viḷiyāṭa variṉ peṟum naṉmi
kūṟalum, vārāviṭiṉ nikaḻun tuṉpaṅ kūṟalum marapu. (cāmam - camamākak kūṟal,
iṉimiyākat kūṟal); ivvēḻu paruvaṅkaḷum iruvakip piḷḷittamiḻiṟkum potu. itaṉ
mēluḷḷa pirivukaḷik kāṇka.
8. (a) ciṟṟil paruvam:- ciṟumi + il - ciṟṟil, ciṟuvīṭu. mūṉṟām
āṇṭiṟkuriyatu. ciṟṟil viḷiyāṭum ciṟumiyar, pāṭṭuṭit talivar tam ciṟṟili
yaḻikkumpōtu "em ciṟṟili aḻiyātoḻika eṉa vēṇṭutal kūṟappaṭum. ciṟumiyar maṇalil
ciṟuvīṭu kaṭṭi nilāccōṟu poṅkuvataṉi āṇ ciṟuvarkaḷ kuṟumpukkāka avaṟṟik kalittuc
ciṟumikaḷ aḻa maṭṭilā makiḻcci koḷvati yāvarum kaṇṭirukkalām. innikaḻcciyi maṉa
nūlār "pāvaṉi viḷiyāṭṭu eṉpar. peṇ kuḻavikaḷic "ciṟuvīṭu kaṭṭuvāyāka" eṉṟu
peṇpāṟ piḷḷittamiḻil kūṟuvatum uṇṭeṉa ikkottiṉ 4, 5. piḷḷittamiḻ nūlkaḷ
terivikkiṉṟaṉa.
9. a. ciṟupaṟipparuvam:- pāṭṭuṭit talivaric ciṟupaṟi muḻakkumpaṭi vēṇṭappeṟum.
itu 2-ām āṇṭil kūṟap peṟuvatu. ipparuvam arumpātti vētavināyakar piḷḷittamiḻil
mutaṟkaṇ vantu piṉṉarē ciṟṟil pāṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉam varuvatu ila. vi. pā. 46- ām
cūttirattukkum varuṭa muṟikkum ottatāyiruppiṉum "ampuli ciṟṟil ciṟupaṟi
ciṟutēr" eṉṟa paṉ pāṭ. cūttirappaṭiyē perumpālum amintuḷatu.
10. a. ciṟutērpparuvam:- ciṟutēr (naṭivaṇṭi) uruṭṭumāṟu pāṭṭuṭit talivari
vēṇṭutal kūṟappeṟum. itu 4-ām āṇṭil kūṟappeṟum.
8. ā. ammāṉipparuvam:- cila piḷḷit tamiḻil itaṟkup patil kaḻaṅkāṭaṟparuvam varum.
kaḻaṅkāvatu maram, īyam mutaliya poruḷkaḷāṉ uruṇṭiyākap pantu pōṉṟu ceyvatu. ataṉi
makaḷir mēlē oṉṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟāka eṟintu kiyāṟ piṭittu viḷiyāṭuvar. kaṭiccaṅka
kālattil kaḻaṅkāṭal mikuntiruntatu eṉpatu ''pantuṅ kaḻaṅkum" (paripāṭal 81.-107)
"kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir polañ cey kaḻaṅkiṟ" (puṟam 36) "muṟṟuṟu kaḻaṅkoṭu mutalakaṭu"
(peruṅ kati pak. 104) eṉpavikaḷālaṟiyalām. ammāṉik kāykaḷ koṇṭu iṅṅaṉam
viḷiyāṭuvatum ipparuvattiṉ taṉmi. itu17-ām mātattil nikaḻum. pāṭṭuṭit taliviyit
tōḻikaḷ muttammāṉi mutaliya ammāṉik kāykoṇṭu āṭiyaruḷumāṟu kūṟuvatākum.
makaḷirukkēyuriya viḷiyāṭṭiṉuḷ iḵtoṉṟu; kalampaka uṟuppukkaḷil ammāṉiyeṉa varuvatu
makaḷir mūvar kūṭit tam nāṭṭut talivaṉi (al) kātalarik kuṟittō orutti viṉā eḻuppa
maṟṟorutti ataṟku viḷakkaṅkūṟa, piṉṉorutti ivviruvarkku viṭi kūṟi ammaṉik
kāykaḷi mēlē vīcippiṭittuk koṇṭu muṭittalāka iṟutic cīril ammāṉiyeṉṟa col varap
pāṭutalākum.
9. ā. nīrāṭaṟparuvam:- itu 19-ām mātam nikaḻum. taliviyi nīr niliyiṟceṉṟu puṉal
viḷiyāṭṭayara vēṇṭumeṉṟu vēṇṭuvatākum. icceyti nalla kaṇavaṉmāriyaṭiya kaṉṉikaḷ
nōṟkum mārkaḻi nōṉpit taḻuviyuḷatu. "vempātāka viyaṉila varippeṉa ampāvāṭaliṉ
āytoṭik kaṉṉiyar ........" eṉavarum paripāṭalāl kaṉṉip peṇkaḷ tāyarukāka niṉṟu
nīrāṭiṉar eṉa viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ''pēṇuñ ciṟappiṉ peṇ makavāyiṉ mūṉṟām āṇṭil kuḻa maṇam
oḻitalum aintiṉ mutalā oṉpatiṉ kāṟum aiṅkaṇik kiḻavaṉi yārvamoṭu nōṟṟalum paṉi nīr
tōytalum pāvi yāṭalum" (piṅkalanti) eṉpatāl 5-9 vayatuḷḷa ciṟu kaṉṉiyarē nīrāṭi
nōṉpu nōṟṟaṟku uriyar eṉpatu pōtarukiṟatu. icceyti 'tāyarukāka niṉṟu" eṉṟa
parimēlaḻakar urikku ēṟṟatāyiṉum civiṟiyāl nīriṟittal, mūḻki viḷiyāṭutal pōṉṟaṉa
vayatu mikunta peṇkaṭkē takkatu. aiṅkuṟu nūṟu 84-ām pāṭṭu, naṟṟiṇi 80ām pāṭṭu,
kalittoki 59-ām pāṭṭu, puṟam 70-ām pāṭṭu, tiruppāvi, tiruvempāvi ākiyaṉa
nīrāṭalippaṟṟi viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟum maliyāḷa nāṭṭup peṇkaḷ aruṇōtaya kālam vari
nīrāṭuvatil kālam pōkkip piṉṉar kariyēṟi naṉku uṭalit tuṭittukkoṇṭu tūymiyāṉa
alaṅkāra uṭi vakikaḷiyaṇintu kaṇkaḷukku miyiṭṭu neṟṟiyil tilatamiṭṭut tāmpūlam
pōṭṭut tammi oppaṉi ceyvar; piṉṉar ūñcalāṭṭattit toṭaṅkuvar. ittaki āṭṭaṅkaḷ
icceytiyāl peṇ kaḷukkēyeṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
10. (ā) ūcaṟparuvam:- itu 21.ām mātam nikaḻum. pāṭṭuṭit taliviyi ūcaloṉṟilēṟi
viḷiyāṭi makiḻumāṟu kūṟum paruvam; aṣṭap pirapantam mutaliyavaṟṟil taṉiyākak kūṟap
peṟuvatu miḵtē.
______________________________

1. arumpātti vētavināyakar piḷḷittamiḻ

"piḷḷiyār piḷḷiyār avar eṅkēyō piḷḷiyār........." eṉṟu ciṟuvar mutal
ciṟumivari pāṭik kaḷikkum perumāṉ vināyakarē. ivar vaḻipāṭu iṅku maṭṭumiṉṟik
kaṭal kaṭanta nāṭukaḷiṉum nēpāḷam, tipet, turukki ākiyavaṟṟiṉum uḷḷatu kuṟippiṭat
takkatu. intiyāvil intukkaḷ maṭṭumiṉṟic camaṇar, puttar ākiyōrum pūcikkiṉṟaṉar.
nantamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkup pallavaṉ taḷapati parañcōtiyār vātāpiyik koḷḷi koṇṭapōtu
aṅkirunta piḷḷiyārik koṇṭu vantu tam ūrāṉa tirucceṅkaṭṭāṅkuṭiyil kaṇapatīccuram
eṉṟa kōyil kaṭṭi eḻuntaruḷac ceytār. icceyti nikaḻntatu ki. pi. 641-42 eṉpar
carittirāciriyar. caṅka kālavilakkiyaṅkaḷil vināyakar paṟṟiya pēccēyillātāl itu mutaṟ
koṇṭē vināyakar vaṇakkam toṭaṅkiyateṉalām. ivar orumukap piḷḷiyār, ōṅkārap piḷḷiyār
eṉap palavitamākap pōṟṟap peṟṟār.
cīṉāvil 'kuṅṣīṉ' eṉṉumiṭattiluḷḷa peḷatta kukik kōyilil cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷa
vināyakaruruvam ki.pi. 31-ām āṇṭic cārntatām. meksikō nakarilum, peruvilum
(amerikkā) kaṇapati aṟuvaṭi kālak kaṭavuḷākap pūcikkappaṭukiṟār. āticaṅkarar intu
matatti virivupaṭuttiyapōtu tāṉ kaṭavuḷariṉ taṉittaṉi vaḻipāṭu ēṟpaṭṭatu. atu toṭaṅki
kaṇapatiyip pūcippavar 'kaṇāpatyar' eṉappaṭṭaṉar. 10-vatu nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉṉarē
tēvikaḷuṭaṉ piḷḷiyār ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭār eṉat terikiṟatu.
ippiḷḷittamiḻ vināyakarip paṟṟiyatātalāl avarip paṟṟiya tutiyō, kāvaṟ kaṭavuḷākak
kūṟappaṭuvatō kāṇappaṭavilli. 15-vatu pāṭṭiṉ kuṟippil nātattileḻupaṟi eṉpatu
apitāṉa cintāmaṇiyil uḷḷa vaṇṇam kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷatu. piṟa nūlkaḷil māṟupāṭu
kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. tirunaḷḷāṟṟiṉiluḷḷa kaṇapatiyip pūcittu naḷaṉ kali nīṅkiṉāṉ eṉṟum
akkaṇapati naḷakūpak kaṇapati eṉṟum 28-vatu pāṭṭu terivikkiṟatu. iṉṟum tirunaḷ
ḷāṟṟiṉiluḷḷa caṉīsvarar kōvilukku makkaḷ ceṉṟu kaḷippatōṭu ‘eḷ kalanta cōṟum’
kākattiṟ kiṭukiṉṟaṉar. tāyar tāmari malarpōṉṟa kikaḷik koṭṭi yaḻittavuṭaṉ
''kuntiyeḻunta pataṅkaḷ cataṅki kuluṅku cilampiriyat tottaṇi muttoṭu muttu
nakikkuḷir cōtinilā viciṟat, tontiyoṭuñ ciṟu paṇṭi yacintiṭu toṅkal puṟañcuḻalak
kittuṇi vittumeṭuttu maṭikkaṭi kāloṭu kālvilakka kāliyiḷaṅkatir pōloḷi poṅkiya
katirmuka metirmarit tattaṭiyiṭṭu ....'' varukiṉṟār vināyakar. orukuḻavi nam
kaṇṇetirē naṭantu varuvatu pōluḷḷatu iccollōviyam; eṉṉē! kavi iṉpam.
vināyakar piṟappiṉi nōkkiya pala purāṇak katikaḷil civapurāṇam pārvati
tāṉirukkumiṭattil āṭavar yārum vārāvaṇṇam kāvalkākka oruvari yēṟpaṭuttiṉāreṉṟum
avarē vināyakar eṉavu miyampukiṟatu. īṇṭu 42-vatu pāṭṭu civaṉum, pārvatiyum
maṇṭapamoṉṟaṉi yaṭintu āṇṭirunta vaṉappi nukarvuḻi, tēvi aṅkup piraṇavap poruḷi nōkka
ataṉil āṇ peṇ yāṉikaḷ kalavi ceyya ataṉik kaṇṭa iṟivi yāteṉalum niṉ campantamāki
orumakaṉ tōṉṟuva" ṉeṉṉak kūṟi civaṉ kaḷiṟṟiṉi nōkki niṟṟalum muṭivil vināyakar
atiṉiṉṟu eḻuntu vantu vaṇaṅkiṉar eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. itaṉik,
''kaṭivi rāviya matampoḻi kaḷiṟṟiṉiyanta
vaṭikaṇōkki niṉṟaṉa ruḷattārvamō ṭammi
piṭiyi nōkki niṉṟaṉa ḷavipēṇiya kalavi
muṭiviṉ mētakat tōṉṟiṉār moyvinā yakarē,"
(vinā. purā.) eṉṟa pāṭṭālaṟiyalām.
46-vatu pāṭṭu tivōtātaṉ eṉkiṟa tivōtāṉaṉ varalāṟṟi virittuk kūṟukiṟatu.
tivōtātaṉ kāciyil tēvarkaḷ irātapaṭi piramaṉiṭam varampeṟṟu aṅṅaṉamē āṭci ceytatāl
mantaramalikku varam koṭukkac ceṉṟa civaṉ kāci tirumpum vaṇṇam muyaṟci ceytum
muṭiyavilli. iṟutiyil kaṇapatiyi yaṉuppa avar cōtiṭaṉāyc ceṉṟu muṟi tiṟampac
ceytār. 'kūrnta nittiriyiṟ ceṉṟu koṭuṅkaṉa vākki yantac, cārnta coppaṉattit tappā
naṉaviṭit tayaṅkac cāṟṟi, nērntiṭu mitaṉukkiṉṉa neṭum payaṉeṉṟu, colli yārntavap
payaṉuṅ kūṭṭi yanēkarit tiṟampac ceytār." (vinā. purā.) piṉṉar ivar kūṟiyavāṟu
tirumāl nimitta murippavaṉāyc ceṉṟu aracaṉic cammatikkac ceyta piṉṉar mantara
maliyiṉiṉṟu civaṉ mutal yāvariyum aḻittiruttiṉar. itaṉiyē "kaṉṉi maṭavār
tamakkumiḷaṅ kāḷiyarukkuṅ kaṉavurittuk kācittalatti mīṇṭu varakkarutum...'' eṉṟaṉar.
itaṉic ceytavar vināyakar eṉpataṉi "... taḻinta kācinakar vāḻkki tantāy nīyē yeṉac cāla
viḻintuṭiṇṭi vināyakari ....'' eṉpataṉāl kāṇka.
vināyarukku 16-peyarkaḷ uṇṭu eṉpataṉi,
“cumukarē yēka tantarē kapilarcūḻ kecakaṉṉarē nāta
mimiḻtaru milam pōtara reḻilvikaṭa rilakiya vikkiṉarāca
ramivarcūḻn tēttuṅ kaṇātipar tūmakētu naṉṟaruḷ kaṇattiyakka rumiḻoḷip pālacantirar
tāṉamūṟ ṟirunteḻuṅ kacāṉaṉarē.''
“vakkiratuṇṭar vayaṅkiya cūrppakaṉṉa rōmpar vāṉ varaṅkār
...kkaruḷ kantapūrvaca reṉavē colliya peyar patiṉāṟu..”
(vinā. purā.) ivikaḷāl 58-ām pāṭṭiṉ peyar vivaram ōrka.
civaṉukkuriyaṉa yāvum vināyakarukkumuṇṭu eṉpati 61-ām pāṭṭu viḷakkukiṟatu.
veṇpiṟik koḻuntik kaṇapati yaṇintaṉar eṉpatu “maruviya kalikaḷuḷ viyakantoḻa vorukali
namatoḷir muṭiyiṉ vikuka....'' (vi. purā.) eṉpatāl viḷaṅkukiṟatu. aravakkalaṉi
yaṇintatu vinatiyiṉ vēṇṭukōḷāl campāti, karuṭaṉ mutaliyavari mīṭṭu varac ceṉṟa
pōtākum.
"irinta paṉṉākamu meytit tāḻntaṉa -
purintavi tammiyum paritta vaṅkatam
purintavaṇ kuḻipuṉi yāra mātiyāt
terintutam muruvelāñ civaṇu vittaṉar.
"tantiyārc cittatē tarum maṉṉati
mintarārc cittalum vaḻakka mātalā
lantivā ṉiṟattava raṇinta vāḷarā
ventiyā raṇitalu mēṟṟa tākumē." (vi. purā.)
eṉpavikaḷāl viḷaṅkak kāṇka.
21- pattiraṅkaḷ koṇṭu vināyakari vaṇaṅka vēṇṭumeṉa vinā. purā. umiyammi caturtti
viratamaṉuṭṭitta paṭalattil 33, 34, 35 pāṭalkaḷil evvep peyarukku eppattiram koṇṭu
pūcikka vēṇṭumeṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. “perumaṇa mācippattiram cumukaṉ ...'' eṉpataṉik
kāṇka. 55-vatu pāṭṭil pūvaṇap piḷḷiyāy eṉa varuvatu aḻakiya niṟam poruntiya
(pū+vaṇam) iḷamip paruvamuṭiyavaṉāy eṉṟum maturikkarukil vikiyāṟ cūḻappeṟṟa ceṭi
niṟinta ūrāṉa tiruppūvaṇattiṉiṉṟu vanta piḷḷiyār eṉṟum poruḷ koḷḷalām; ēṭakam eṉṟa
talattil tāṉ, viki nīril etirnta ēṭu kariyēṟiyatu. itu vikiyiṉ vaṭakariyiluḷḷatu.
aṟiñar ārāyka.
''piṉpu meḻil kuṉṟāta piḷḷiyāreṉa varum piḷḷiyiva...'' eṉpatil piḷḷiyār eṉṟa
peyar peṟṟatu piḷḷip paruvac ciṟuvarkaḷ catā eḻuttu vēli ceyvatu pōṉṟu ivarum
mērumaliyil taṉṉoru tantatti yoṭittup pāratam eḻutiyatālēyē eṉak karuṇīka purāṇam
iyampukiṟatu.
69- ām pāṭṭu vināyakarē piraṇavap poruḷ eṉa iyampukiṟatu; ''piraṇava muṉṉar vikum
perumaṟi tokuttakāli, piraṇava muṉṉōtāmaṟ pēcutaliṉmiyālē, piraṇava moḻiyāṟ ṟaṉṉip
pēciṉi yeṉavuṭkoṇṭu piraṇavapporuḷāyuḷḷāṉ pēriṭar kāttāṉaṉṟē.'' ''vaḷarōṅkārat
turukkoṇṭa vaḷḷalivaṉē ...'' (vinā. purā.) eṉpaṉa ōrka.
vināyakar uruva vaḻipāṭu iṉpa nikaḻcci (a) tuṉpanikaḻcciyā toṉṟaṟkum
iṉṟiyamiyātatu. “vittāl piḷḷiyār vaḻitteṟintāl cāṇi" “vellattil piḷḷiyār
ceytu atilēyē kiḷḷi nivētyañ ceytāṟpōla'' eṉṟa paḻamoḻikaḷi nōkka vināyakaric
cāṇiyiṉālum vellattiṉālum maṟṟuñ can taṉam, mañcaḷ, maṇ mutaliyavikaḷālum ceytu
vaṇaṅkalāmeṉṟu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ittakiya eḷiya celavil vittu vaṇaṅkum kaṭavuḷar vēṟu
yāvar! pāṟkaṭal kaṭiyum pōtu vināyakari vaṇaṅkātatāl ālakāla viṣam piṟakka intiraṉ
mutaliyōr kaṭal nuriyāl tiruvalañcuḻiyil piḷḷiyār ceytu vaṇaṅkiṉarām, (75-ām
pāṭṭu); ivveḷḷip piḷḷiyārkkut tutikki yātu kāraṇattālō valap pakkamāka uḷatu.
ittakiyavaric civaṉum pārvatiyum taṅkaḷ tirumaṇak kālaṅkaḷilē vittu vaṇaṅkāmalilli.
civaṉum tiripura caṅkārattiṉ pōtu vināyakarit tutiyātatāl tēriṉ accu mutaliyaṉa
muṟiyap piṉṉar tutittuc cariyāṉatāka pāṭṭu iyampukiṟatu.
81-vatu pāṭṭāl eṅkuc celliṉum yātoṉṟaṟkum vināyakari niṉittē kāriyan toṭaṅkuvar
eṉpatu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. muṟkālattil intiya viyāpārikaḷ kaṭaṟ pirayāṇam ceyyumpōtu
puyal, kappal koḷḷiyar tollikaḷ mutaliyaṉa ēṟpaṭāmaliruppataṟkākak kappalil piḷḷiyār
vikkirakatti eṭuttuc celvatu vaḻakkamām. ivarkaṭkut tuṉpam nēriṉ ivvikkirakattit
talikīḻāka vipparām. nam vīṭṭiluṅkūṭa iṅṅaṉamāṉāl vikkirakatti nīril pōṭap pārkkiṟōm.
tuṉpam nēriṭāmal kāppāṟṟāta kaṇapatikku iḵtōr ēṟṟa taṇṭaṉi pōlum! eṉavētāṉ "kaṭalilē
vaṅka mēṟic celliṉum '' eṉṟār.
“pantuṅ kaḷapap paṉic ceppum " eṉṟa 25-vatu pāṭṭu mātarkaḷ taṅkaḷi vantu taḻuvum
kaṇavarip pārttu "itō tēṉiṟāl oṉṟuḷatu; atip piṭittu vaṭittut tarutir " eṉṟu
kūṟuvataṟku "....... neytaluṅ kumutappūvu nekiḻnta ceṅkamalappōtuṅ kikaḷu mukamum
vāyuṅ kaṇkaḷuṅ kāṭṭak kaṇṭu koytivi tarutireṉṟu koḻunarit toḻukiṉṟārāl."
(eḻuc. paṭa. 54) eṉṟa kam. irā. ceyyuḷi ēṟakkuṟiya oppumi kūṟalām. 97-ām
pāṭṭiluḷḷa "vāṉēṟu ciṉiyēṟi mantik kulaṅkaḷāl '' eṉpatu
"antara nivantacōli yalarpacuṅ kōṭṭiṟāl kaṇ
mantika ḷukaḷun tōṟuṅ kiḻintu vārmatu nīr pāyntu
centiṉi viḷikkuñ...'' (vinā. purā)
eṉpataṉi yottal kāṇka,
tamiḻaṉpu :- ivvāciriyar vināyaka purāṇam, kamparāmāyaṇam ākiyavaṟṟil payiṟci
mikkavarāvatōṭu vaṭamoḻi, teṉmoḻi ākiyavaṟṟiyum palapaṭappukaḻkiṉṟavar. kāmēvu miyalici
nāṭakameṉu muttamiḻiṉ kaṭali (cey 5) tiṇi centamiḻk koḷu (9) mēlāṉa vaṭacolum teṉ
coluṅ kaṟṟa mēlōr (11) mūvakit tamiḻāṭa (13) kōtaṟṟa vaṭamoḻiyō (15) teyvat
tamiḻkoḷu (16) vīṟutamiḻ koṇṭu (55) meyttamiḻaḷittiṭum (56) centamiḻiṉ
vaḷamipeṟu (70) cintiyurukip pintamiḻ nūl (86) ākiyavikaḷālaṟiyalām.
92-ām pāṭṭil irāmaṉ, cīti, ilakkumaṇaṉ ākiyavarkaḷip paṭikaḷōṭu tēril ēṟṟi vantu
ayōttiyil cērttaṉar eṉpatu vinā. purā. viṣṇuvip paṟṟiya purāṇaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭāc
ceyti. purāṇaṅkaḷilillāta ceyti talapurāṇaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭuvatuṇṭu eṉa maccapurāṇam
patippāciriyar mukavuriyil kūṟiyataṉpaṭi talapurāṇam itaṟkuṇṭō eṉṟa aiyam nērukiṟatu.
varalāṟu teriyātatālum orē piratiyātalālum aiyamāṉa viṭattil mūlattiluḷḷavāṟē
patippikkappaṭṭuḷatu. avviyamāṉa varikaḷ kīḻkkaṇṭavāṟumirukkalāmō eṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
''viṇṭēril ēṟi ikal veṉṟu mātaraciyoṭu
mīmiyiru tēril nāṉa
veṟṟimaka ṉumvīr kattiviṭa vēmaṇi
viyaṉ tēr iṟaṅki maṇinūl
paṇṭēr vaṉattiṉil paṇiyumnāḷ appēr
paṭittu ....''
(mī+mi+ irutēril - mēlēcellum mēkam pōṉṟa periya puṭpaka vimāṉattil; nāṉa veṟṟi
makaṉ - ñāṉattiyum eṅkuc celliṉum veṟṟiyiyumuṭiya aṉumāṉ; appērpaṭittu -
irāmaṉatu peyariyum cērttu vināyakar peṟṟu.) irāmēsvaram tavira
tiruvucāttāṉattilum irāmar, cukkirīpaṉ, cāmpuvāṉ ākiyōr paṇintatākat tēvāram
kūṟukiṟatu. maṟṟum tirunāḷip pōvār puṅkūril kuḷañcamittatil kūliyip
pakirntaḷittaṉar vināyakar eṉpatu periya purāṇattil kāṇappaṭātatu.
ittaki ciṟanta nūliṉ āciriyar peyar aṟiya muṭiyāmaliruppatu varuntattakkatu.
iruppiṉum innūlil 19-vatu, 29-vatu pāṭalkaḷiṉiṉṟu ciṟcila kuṟippukkaḷ
kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.
ivvāciriyar vaṟumip pēyāl vañcikkappaṭṭup putuccelvam tōṉṟiyār kaṇṇellām ceṉṟu
intirā, cantirā eṉṟu kūṟi niṉṟavarām. ivariṭam ivvināyakar taṉatu pātatti
maṟavātirukkumāṟu kūṟi yāṭkoṇṭaṉarām (cey. 19). ivvāciriyarkku iru piḷḷikaḷ
piṟantaṉareṉṟum vētavināyakarē kuruvāka vantu tīṭci purintāreṉṟum appiḷḷikaṭkuc
cuppiramaṇiyaṉ, vētavināyakaṉ (al) ikkaṭavuḷarkkuriya peyari vittaṉa reṉṟum neñcil
niṉittavikaḷi yellām vināyakar koṭuttaṉareṉṟum tāy, tanti, kuru, teyvam ākiyavarāy
viḷaṅkiṉareṉṟum 29-ām pāṭṭu kūṟukiṟatu.
ivvarumpātti teṉ āṟkāṭu māvaṭṭattil tirukkōvalūr vaṭṭattiluḷḷa ōr ciṟiya ūrākum
eṉat terikiṟatu. iv vaṭṭattiluḷḷa ūr puḷḷi vivarappaṭṭiyal puttakattil (Village
Statistics Register) 'arumpāttu' eṉak kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷatu. eṅkaḷiṭam uḷḷa ōr
ōliccuvaṭiyil ivvināyakarmītu pāṭiya kīrttaṉattil "arumpātti vētavināyakar '' eṉak
kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷatu. ivikaḷi nōkkiṉ ivvūr arumpātti, arumpātti, arumpāttu
eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷateṉa aṟiyalām. iṅku vētavināyakar kōyiloṉṟē yuḷatu eṉpatu kāppuc
ceyyuḷil tala civapirāṉ, umi ākiyavarmītu pāṭalkaḷ kāṇappaṭātatāl uyttuṇaralām.
vināyakaṉ, viṉāyakaṉ iraṇṭiyum pētamiṉṟi yāḷuvatu ōlippiratiyil kaṇṭavuṇmi.
ippiratiyil cila pāṭaṅkaḷ mūlattiluḷḷavāṟē patippikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ippiḷḷittamiḻ mūvaruṭak kāṭalākku 2677 - ām eṇṇiṉiṉṟu veḷiyiṭappeṟukiṟatu. itu
teṉṉāṟkāṭu māvaṭṭam, kumarakuppam kirāmattic cērnta tiru. K. ṣaṇmukam avarkaḷāl
naṉkoṭiyāka aḷikkappaṭṭatākum.
